### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

An alloy of Pb-Ag weighing 1.08 g was dissolved in dilute <math><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> and the volume made to 100 mL. A silver electrode was dipped in the solution and the emf of the cell set-up, Pt(s), <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>M</mtext><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mtext>A</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo></math> was 0.62 V. If <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> is 0.80 V, what is the percentage of Ag in the alloy? (At <math><mn>25</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> , RT/F = 0.06)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **50**

**Detailed Solution:**

Overall cell reaction is
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>A</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2.303</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mn>0.62</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.80</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.06</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2.303</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math>
<math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0.05</mn></math> M
Number of moles of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> in 100 mL <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.05</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.005</mn></math>
Mass of silver <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.005</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>108</mn></math> g
Percentage of Ag in 1.08 g of alloy <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.005</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>108</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1.08</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">%</mi></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

When copper sulphate solution is electrolysed in a copper voltameter for 30 sec, then m gram of copper was deposited. Time current graph for the electrolysis is

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/f28411b2-a5a7-4ee7-bb87-0ba1d1ccbc42.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 250px; height: 147px;" />

The electrochemical equivalent of copper from above plot will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** Z = m
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

m = ZIt 
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>  
It = Total charge passed through copper voltameter 
= Area under the curve 
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>  
So <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A current of <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.93</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>ampere</mtext></mrow> </math> is passed through <math> <mrow> <mtext>200</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mL</mtext></mrow> </math> of <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.5</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Zinc</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>sulphate</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>(aq</mtext><mtext>.)</mtext></mrow> </math> solution for <math> <mrow> <mtext>50</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>min</mtext></mrow> </math> with a current efficiency of <math> <mrow> <mtext>80%</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow> </math> If volume of solution remain constant, then <math><mo>[</mo><mtext>Z</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2+</mtext></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></math> after deposition of <math><mtext>Z</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2+</mtext></mrow></msup></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.38 M
- **(B)** 0.26 M
- **(C)** 0.35 M
- **(D)** 0.076 M

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 0.38 M**

**Detailed Solution:**

No. of Faraday <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1.93</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>50</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.06</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Moles of <math><mtext>Z</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2+</mtext></mrow></msup></math> deposited <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>80</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.024</mn></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2234;</mo><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfenced close="]" open="[">   <mrow>    <msup>     <mrow>      <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow>     <mrow>      <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow>    </msup>    </mrow>  </mfenced><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.5</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00D7;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.024</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.38</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>M</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the following electrochemical cell at 298 K,

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.092</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> when <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup></math>

Given: <math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mo>|</mo><msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.151</mn><mtext>V</mtext><mo>;</mo><mn>2.303</mn><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>RT</mtext></mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.059 </mn><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

The value of x is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo></math> 2
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo></math> 1
- **(C)** 1
- **(D)** 2

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 2**

**Detailed Solution:**

Anode:           <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> 

Cathode:         <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> 

                 <math><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> 

 <math><mn>0.092</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.151</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo></mrow></mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> 

 <math><mn>0.092</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.151</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The temperature coefficient of the emf i.e., <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>dE</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>dt</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mo>–</mo><mn>0.00065</mn><msup> <mrow> <mtext> VK</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo> </mo></mrow> </math> for the cell <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> at <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>25</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext></mrow> </math>. Calculate the entropy changes <math> <mrow> <mtext>ΔS</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> for the cell reaction <math> <mrow> <mtext>Cd</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>AgCl</mtext><mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cd</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cl</mtext></mrow> <mo>–</mo> </msup> <mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow> </math> .

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>105.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>150.2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>75.7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>125.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>–</mo><mn>125.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>0.00065</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mo>&#x2013;</mo><mn>125.5</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>JK</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2013;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<math> <mrow> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⟶</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Four successive members of the first row transition elements are listed below with atomic numbers. Which one of them is expected to have to highest  <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> , value?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>26</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.41</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.57</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.77</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.97</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> 

Standard reduction potential (SRP) value normally increases from left to right in the period of d-block elements. Some SRP values are exceptionally higher due to stability of the product ion. For e.g., 

 <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.57</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.97</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> .

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> for reaction, <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> is 2.73 V. If <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> are -157 kJ <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and -237.2 kJ <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> determine <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> for <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** -1580 kJ
- **(B)** -1303 kJ
- **(C)** -1260 kJ
- **(D)** -1380 kJ

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) -1303 kJ**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c20ca66a-da38-45c3-abc3-f3d102e1e898.png" style="width:2.08333in;height:0.89583in" />
For given cell reaction,
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>nE</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext>F</mtext></mrow> </math>
So, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>12</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.73</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>96500</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>J</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>3.1613</mn><mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>kJ</mtext></mrow> </math>
Now for given reactions,
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>f</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mtext>Al</mtext><msub> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>OH</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>4</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mtext>6</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>f</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mo>−</mo><mtext>4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>f</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>OH</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
(Also note that <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> for elements is zero)
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>3.1613</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>237.2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>157</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
So, <math> <mrow>  <msubsup>   <mtext>G</mtext>   <mtext>f</mtext>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msubsup>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <mtext>Al</mtext><msub>       <mrow>        <mfenced>         <mrow>          <mtext>OH</mtext></mrow>        </mfenced></mrow>       <mtext>4</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mo>&#x2212;</mo>  </msup>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>1303</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>kJ</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The emf of the cell <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>0.01</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>0.001</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo></math> Fe at 298 K is 0.2905 volt. Then the value of equilibrium constant for the cell reaction is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0.32</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>0.0295</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.32</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>0.0295</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.26</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>0.0295</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.32</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.32</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>0.0295</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>E</mtext><mo>=</mo><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.0591</mn></mrow> <mtext>n</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext>log</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Q</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mn>0.02905</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.01</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.001</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.2905</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.0295</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.32</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>volt</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mtext>E</mtext>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>0.0591</mn></mrow>   <mtext>n</mtext>  </mfrac>  <mtext>log</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>eq</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mn>0.32</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>0.0591</mn></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  <mtext>log</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>eq</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mn>0.02945</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>log</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>eq</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>eq</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>0.32</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>0.0295</mn></mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The following reaction occurs during rusting of iron

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>2H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2e</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>→</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> =</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+1</mtext><mtext>.23</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2e</mtext><mo>→</mo><mtext>Fe</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>(s);</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> =</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.44</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>(s)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>2H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>2H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>2e</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>→</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O(l);</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The measured voltage of the cell, <math> <mrow> <mtext>Pt</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>(s)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1.0</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mtext>(q)</mtext><mo>|</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1.0</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Ag</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>(s)</mtext></mrow> </math> is 1.02 V at.298 K and <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> is 0.80 V, Find the pH of the solution.
Report your answer by rounding it upto nearest whole number.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext></mrow> <mtext>n</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>log</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>[H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext>]</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>[Ag</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext>]</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>log</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>−</mo><mn>0.059</mn><mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>log</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo><mn>0.059</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mo>−</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>log[H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext>]</mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> <mn>1</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo><mn>0.059</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>pH</mtext></mrow> </math>
So <math> <mrow> <mtext>pH</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mo>−</mo><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.02</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.80</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>3</mtext><mtext>.728</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which graph correctly correlates <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub></math> as a function of concentrations for the cell (for different values of M and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></math> .
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1.10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>:</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>:</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/a146bd39-b6de-43d9-81e5-0573a3f872cc.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.225in;height:1.19167in">
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c7adb137-c786-4e07-8e0f-b9c0991d82b4.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.225in;height:1.19167in" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/41167753-9871-4c1a-991e-e87997a54aad.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.24167in;height:1.19167in">
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/bf443e56-efe0-4536-8be5-65e98f289929.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.24167in;height:1.19167in">

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c7adb137-c786-4e07-8e0f-b9c0991d82b4.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.225in;height:1.19167in" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

According to Nernst equation
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
using <math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
Thus, slope is negative.

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For an electrochemical cell, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>aq</mtext><mo>,</mo><mtext>1 M</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>aq</mtext><mo>,</mo><mtext>1 M</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mtext>Pb</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> , the ratio <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2+</mtext></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math> when this cell attains equilibrium is

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>Given: </mtext><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>|</mo><mtext>Sn</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.14</mn><mtext>V, </mtext><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>|</mo><mtext>Pb</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.13</mn><mtext>V,</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mtext> RT</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.06</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 4.3
- **(B)** 1
- **(C)** -2.15
- **(D)** 2.14

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 2.14**

**Detailed Solution:**

At equilibrium
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mtext>cell</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mtext>cell</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.01</mn><mtext> V</mtext></math>
<math><mtext>Sn</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2+</mtext></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2+</mtext></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>Pb</mtext></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mtext>cell</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mtext>cell</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.01</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mn>0.01</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>P</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>b</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msup>       <mrow>        <mtext>Sn</mtext></mrow>       <mrow>        <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow>      </msup>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msup>       <mrow>        <mtext>Pb</mtext></mrow>       <mrow>        <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow>      </msup>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mrow>    <mfrac>     <mn>1</mn>     <mn>3</mn>    </mfrac>    </mrow>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mn>2.14</mn></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A beaker containing mixture of <math> <mrow>  <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>M</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>Y</mtext></mrow></math> and <math> <mrow>  <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>M</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>Z</mtext></mrow></math>. A gas <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> at <math><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> is bubbled through this solution at <math><mtext>2</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>5</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> . If the reduction potential of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> , then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> will oxidize  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> and not  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> will oxidize  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math> and not  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> will oxidize both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math>  will reduce both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> will oxidize  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> and not  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

From the reduction potential, the tendency to gain electron is in the order  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> 

Thus  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><msup><mrow><mo> </mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> ; <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> .

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

How long (approximate) should water be electrolysed by passing through 100 amperes current so that the oxygen released can completely burn 27.66 g of diborane?
(Atomic weight of B <math><mo>=</mo></math> 10.8 u)

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.6 hours
- **(B)** 6.4 hours
- **(C)** 0.8 hours
- **(D)** 3.2 hours

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 3.2 hours**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>  
According to balanced equation 
27.66 g <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math> i.e. 1 mole <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math> requires 3 mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> . Now this oxygen is produced by electrolysis of water as follows 
<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mover accent="false"><mo>→</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>  
As 1 mole <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is produced by 4 F charge 
So 3 mole <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> will be produced by 12 F charge 
So now on applying 
Q <math><mo>=</mo></math> It 
<math><mtext>12</mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext>965</mtext><mn>00</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3600</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> hours 
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3.2</mn></math> hours

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

How long will it take for a uniform current of 6.00 A to deposit 78 g of gold from a solution of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> ? What mass of chlorine gas will be formed simultaneously at anode of the cell? 
(Atomic mass of Au = 197)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3010</mn></math> sec, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>35.50</mn></math> g
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20306</mn></math> sec, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>45.54</mn></math> g
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>19500</mn></math> sec, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>54.5</mn></math> g
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>19139.16</mn></math> sec, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>42.24</mn></math> g

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>19139.16</mn></math> sec, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>42.24</mn></math> g**

**Detailed Solution:**

Reactions take place at electrodes are 
At cathode <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>  
At cathode <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>  
For the deposition of 197 g (1 mole) of Au = 3F of charge is required thus, for the deposition of 78 g of Au, charge required 
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>197</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>78</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1.19</mn><mtext>F</mtext></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1.19</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></math> coulumbs 
From <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1.19</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>19139.16</mn></math> sec 
By 1 F charge, 35.5 g of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> gas is formed, thus from 1.19 F, mass of chlorine gas formed <math><mo>=</mo><mn>35.5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1.19</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>42.24</mn></math> g

</details>

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">Δ</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>G</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>o</mtext></mrow></msup></math> for the following cell reaction
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mtext>Zn</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>l</mtext> </mfenced><mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>Ag</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup> <mrow> <mtext>OH</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>A</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mtext>A</mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.80</mn><mtext>V</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">a</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">n</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">d</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>Z</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mtext>Z</mtext><mtext>n</mtext></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.76</mn><mtext>V ,F=96500</mtext></math>

(Given: <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>sp</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext>of</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>AgOH</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>10</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>305</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>212</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>305</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>301</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>212</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Calculation for <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
Reduction <math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>∆</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>×</mo><msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></msubsup></math>
Ionisation <math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>∆</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msub></math>
Net <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo></math> <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>∆</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>×</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>

<math><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>×</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>×</mo><msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></math>

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate the equilibrium constant for the reaction (in 10<sup>12</sup>)

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>⇌ </mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

Given, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo> = </mo> <msup> <mtext>1.44 V,    E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo> = </mo> <mtext>0.68 V</mtext> </mrow> </math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mfrac><mi>RT</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>.</mo><mn>0591</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **7.07**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>⇌</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>E</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Ce</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mo> -  </mo> <mtext>E</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

    <math><mrow><mo> = </mo> <mtext>1.44</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>0.68</mtext> <mo> = </mo> <mtext>0.76 V</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∵    </mo><msup><mtext>E</mtext><mtext>o</mtext></msup><mo> = </mo><mtext>0.059</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"> log </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒    </mo> <mtext mathvariant="italic"> log </mtext> <mtext>K</mtext> <mo> = </mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>0.0592</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                    <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>= </mo><mfrac><mtext>0.76</mtext><mtext>0.059</mtext></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mtext>12.85</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒     </mo><mtext>K</mtext><mo> = </mo><mtext>7.07</mtext><mo>×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

When 0.04 F of electricity is passed through a solution of CaSO<sub>4</sub>, then the weight of Ca<sup>2</sup><sup>+</sup> metal deposited at the cathode is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.2 g
- **(B)** 0.4 g
- **(C)** 0.6 g
- **(D)** 0.8 g

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 0.8 g**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ca</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>-</mo> </msup> <mo>&rarr;</mo> <mtext>Ca ​</mtext></mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>Ca</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>w</mtext> <mtext>Ca</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>Ca</mtext> </msub> <mo>&times;</mo> <mtext>No. of faradays</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mtext>0.04</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.8 g</mtext> </mrow> </math>.

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the following cell reaction

<math><mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mtext>O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>q</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>q</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>O</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

If <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>l</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>o</mtext></mrow></msubsup></math> at <math><msup><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext>o</mtext></mrow></msup><mtext>C</mtext></math> and <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>F</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>M</mtext><mo>,</mo></math><math><mtext> </mtext><msub><mtext>P</mtext><mrow><msub><mtext>O</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></msub></math><math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.01</mn></math> atm and <math><mtext>p</mtext><mtext>H</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext>x</mtext></math>

Value of <math><mtext>x</mtext></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1
- **(B)** 2
- **(C)** 3
- **(D)** 4

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 1**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi>cell</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi>cell</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>

log <math><mtext>Q</mtext></math> must be zero

<math><mtext>Q</mtext><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.01</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>
