### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

0.02 equivalent of Ag was deposited in an electrolysis experiment. If same quantity of a electricity is passed through a gold solution, 1.314 g of gold is deposited. Find oxidation state of the gold. (Atomic mass of Au = 197)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mtext>A</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><munder><mrow><mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>1 mol </mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow></munder></math> 

0.02 q. of Ag = 0.02 F = 0.02  <math><mo>×</mo></math>  96500 = 1930 C

1.314 Au is deposited by 0.02 F

197 is deposited by  <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.02</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1.314</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>197</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The standard electrode potentials, <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math> of <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mrow></math> at 300 K are +0.77 V and -0.44 V, respectively. The <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math> of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mrow></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext>/Fe</mtext></mrow> </math> at the same temperature is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.21 V
- **(B)** 0.33 V
- **(C)** -0.036 V
- **(D)** 0.036

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) -0.036 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>3+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>F</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.77</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><mtext>Fe;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>F</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.44</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>3+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mn>3</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><mtext>Fe;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mtext>F</mtext><mo>×</mo><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>=0</mtext><mtext>.11</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2234;</mo><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mtext>E</mtext>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>0.11</mn></mrow>   <mn>3</mn>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>0.036</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Copper sulphate solution <math> <mrow> <mtext>(250</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mL)</mtext></mrow> </math> was electrolysed using a platinum anode and a copper cathode. A constant current of <math> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mA</mtext></mrow> </math> was passed for <math> <mrow> <mtext>16</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>min</mtext></mrow> </math>. It was found that after electrolysis the absorbance of the solution was reduced to <math> <mrow> <mtext>50%</mtext></mrow> </math> of its original value. Concentration of copper sulphate in the solution to begin with is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>. Find the valu of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

The number of faraday's passed <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                                                     <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.99</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo> </mrow> </math> number of gram equivalent of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cu</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math> deposited <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>as Cu(s)</mtext> <mrow><mn></mn><mo></mo></mrow></msup></mrow></math>

                                               <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.99</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo> </mrow> </math> number of moles of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cu</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math> deposited

                         <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>1.99</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>≈</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

Absorbance is directly proportional to <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cu</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>.

No. of moles of Cu<sup>2</sup><sup>+</sup> initially present = 2 x 10<sup>-5</sup> moles

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cu</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>Initial</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                   = 8 x10<sup>-5</sup>M

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following is not correct?

**Options:**

- **(A)** The metallic conduction is due to the movement of electrons in the metal
- **(B)** The electrolytic conduction is due to the movement of ions in the solution
- **(C)** The current carrying ions are not necessarily discharged at the electrodes
- **(D)** The metallic conduction increases with the increase in temperature, whereas that of electrolytic conduction decreases with temperature.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) The metallic conduction increases with the increase in temperature, whereas that of electrolytic conduction decreases with temperature.**

**Detailed Solution:**

The metallic conduction decreases with increase in temperature, but electrolytic conduction increases with the increase in temperature. Because the metallic conduction is due to the movement of electrons in the metal and the electrolytic conduction is due to the movement of ions in the solution.

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The standard electrode potentials <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> of four metals <math> <mrow>  <mtext>A,</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>B,</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>C</mtext></mrow></math> and <math> <mtext>D</mtext></math> are <math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V,</mtext></mrow></math>, <math> <mrow>  <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.6</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>, <math> <mrow>  <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.85</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math> and <math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.76</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>, respectively. The sequence of deposition of metals on applying potential is

**Options:**

- **(A)** B &gt; D &gt; C &gt; A
- **(B)** A &gt; C &gt; B &gt; D
- **(C)** C &gt; B &gt; D &gt; A
- **(D)** D &gt; A &gt; B &gt; C

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) C &gt; B &gt; D &gt; A**

**Detailed Solution:**

In complete cation electrolysis at cathode, the species having higher reduction potential is preferably reduced at cathode
<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>&gt;</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>&gt;</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>D</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>&gt;</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>
Hence,  C &gt; B &gt; D &gt; A
will be the preference of deposition of these metals on competeting.

</details>

  Ni(s) + 2 Ag<sup>+</sup> (0.002 M)  <math> <mrow> <mo>⟶</mo> </mrow> </math> Ni<sup>2</sup><sup>+</sup> (0.160 M) + 2 Ag(s)

Given that <math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>cell</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mtext> V</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.91**

**Detailed Solution:**

From the given cell reaction and nernst equation.

                 <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>cell</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>cell</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>n</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> log </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ni</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                       <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mtext> V</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> log </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                       <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> log </mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>4</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

                       <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

                       <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>1</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext><mn>9</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mtext> V</mtext> </mrow> </math>

               <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>cell</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>9</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mtext> V</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><msub><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow></msub></math> . If  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><msub><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the cell reaction,  <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> is 1.89 V. If  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><msub><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow></msub></math>   <math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0.28</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1.64</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1.64</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2.08</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2.17</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1.64</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In this cell  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></math> is oxidised and it acts as anode and Ce acts as cathode.

 <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mtext>1.89</mtext><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mtext>0.28</mtext><mo>)</mo></math> 

 <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mtext>1.89</mtext><mo>-</mo><mtext>0.28</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext>1.61</mtext></math> Volts.

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

When an electric current is passed through acidulated, water, 112 mL of hydrogen gas at NTP collects at the cathode in 965 seconds. The current passed, in amperes, is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

22,400 mL of hydrogen at STP (or NTP) = 2g

So 112 mL of hydrogen at STP  <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>112</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22,400</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> 

 <math><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></munder><mo>→</mo><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> mol</mtext></mrow></munder></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> 

2 g hydrogen is deposited by  <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> 

So  <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>  g hydrogen will be deposited by

 <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>965</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> 

Charge  <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math> 

So  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>965</mn><mtext> C</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>965</mn><mtext> s</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext> C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A solution of Ni(NO<sub>3</sub>)<sub>2</sub> is electrolyzed between platinum electrodes using a current of 5 amperes for 20 min. What mass of Ni is deposited at the cathode ? (Atomic mass of Ni = 58.7)
[Report your answer by rounding it upto nearset whole number]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

Quantity of electricity used = Current in amperes x Time in second = 5A x (20 x 60) s = 6000 C

Chemical equation for the reaction during electrolysis :

                       <math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>Ni</mtext> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>NO</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>-</mo> </msup> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⟶</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>Ni</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>HNO</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>F</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>5</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>7</mn> <mtext>g</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

Charge Q on n moles of electrons is given by : Q = nF

Charge required to deposit 1 mole nickel

                                    = 2 F = 2 x 96500 C = 1.93 x 10<sup>5</sup> C

Molar mass of nickel = 58.7g mol<sup>-1</sup>

1.93 x 10<sup>5</sup> C of charge produces nickel = 58.7g

6000 C of charge produces nickel <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>7 </mn> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>×</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0 </mn> <mtext>C</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>9</mn> <mn>3</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mtext> C</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                                                      <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>8</mn> <mn>2</mn><mtext> g</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The standard reduction potentials at 298 K for single electrodes are given below.<table border="1">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td>Electrode</td>			<td>electrode potential (volt)</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Mg</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Mg</mtext></mrow> </math></td>			<td><math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.34</mtext></mrow> </math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Zn</mtext></mrow> </math></td>			<td><math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.76</mtext></mrow> </math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Fe</mtext></mrow> </math></td>			<td><math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.44</mtext></mrow> </math></td>		</tr>	</tbody></table>From this we can tell that

**Options:**

- **(A)** Zn can reduce both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** Fe can reduce both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** Mg can reduce both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** Mg can reduce  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> but not  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Mg can reduce both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Metals with lower SRP can displace the metal with higher SRP from their solutions. Mg with lower SRP (-2.34) can reduce both  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  (-0.76) and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  (-0.44).

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The voltage of the cell consisting of  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></math>  and  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></math>  electrodes is 5.92 V at standard condition at 298 K. What is the voltage if the electrolyte consists of 2 M LiF. 

(ln2 = 0.693, R = 8.314 J  <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>  and F = 96500 C  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** 5.90 V
- **(B)** 5.937 V
- **(C)** 5.88 V
- **(D)** 4.9 V

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 5.90 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></math> 

 <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>5.92</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> 

 <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5.92</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>5.92</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.059</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.3010</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5.90</mn></math> V

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Given the standard potential for the following half-cell reaction at 298 K,

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0.52</mn></math> V

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0.16</mn></math> V

Calculate the <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo></math> (kJ) for the reaction, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** -34.740
- **(B)** -65.720
- **(C)** -69.480
- **(D)** -131.440

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) -34.740**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0.52</mn></math> V

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.52</mn></math> ...(i)

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0.16</mn></math> V

or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.16</mn></math> V

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0.16</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> ...(ii)

On adding equations (i) and (ii), we get

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> ,

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.52</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.16</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>96500</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0.52</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.16</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.36</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math> <mrow>  <mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>34740&#x00A0;J</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>34</mtext><mtext>.740</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>kJ</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3.13</mn><mtext>V</mtext></math>
The correct plot of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub></math> versus <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> will be represented as

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/7dfa5527-3d65-4205-bb86-7f3d37d34129.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.625in;height:1.475in">
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/120cfdc3-05ef-4809-a640-95752412b6ff.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.56667in;height:1.475in" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/16d105e7-af71-4b95-a275-607687c37436.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.55833in;height:1.475in">
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/506c2cd8-36ff-4621-bb89-a4d5cce14b98.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.50833in;height:1.475in">

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/120cfdc3-05ef-4809-a640-95752412b6ff.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.56667in;height:1.475in" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

For the given cell,
<math> <mrow> <mtext>E</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>log</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>10</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Mg</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
This equation is of the form <math> <mrow> <mtext>y</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mx</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>c,</mtext></mrow> </math> its slope is negative and intercept equal to <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A fuel cell involves combustion of the butane at at 1 atm and 298 K
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>10</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>4</mn><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2744</mn></math> kJ/mole
The value of <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> 
Report your answer by rounding it upto nearest whole number.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>10</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>4</mn><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mfenced></math>
Here total change in oxidation number of C-atoms <math><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn></math>
It means total number of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> involved = 26
As <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2744</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>×</mo><mn>1000</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>26</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.09</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
<math><mo>≈</mo><mn>1.0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

</details>

<math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msubsup> <mtext>ClO</mtext> <mn>3</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> <mo>⇌</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>ClO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>ClO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the following cell,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
when the concentration of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> is 10 times the concentration of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> ,
the expression for <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></math> (in <math> <mrow> <mtext>J</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>) is
[F is Faraday constant; R is gas constant; T is temperature; E°(cell) = 1.1 V]

- **(B)** 1.1 F
- **(C)** 2.303 RT - 2.2 F
- **(D)** - 2.2 F

<math> <mrow>  <mtext>&#x0394;G</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x0394;G&#x00B0;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.303</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>RT</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>log</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mtext>10</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>Q;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>Q</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msup>       <mrow>        <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow>       <mrow>        <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow>      </msup>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msup>       <mrow>        <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow>       <mrow>        <mtext>2</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo></mrow>      </msup>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>2F</mtext><mfenced>   <mrow>    <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.1</mtext></mrow>  </mfenced><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.303</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>RT</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>log</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mtext>10</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>10</mtext></mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.303</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>RT</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>F</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

A current of 2 amp when passed for 5 hour through a molten salt deposits 22.2 g of metal of atomic mass 177. The oxidation state of the metal in the metal salt is
[Report your answer by rounding it upto nearset whole number]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>22.2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3600</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>59.5</mn></math>
Equivalent mass <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c </mi><mtext>mass</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>59.5</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>177</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
So <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>≈</mo><mn>3</mn></math> .

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A 1.0 M solution of Cd<sup>2+</sup> is added to excess iron and the system is allowed to reach equilibrium. What is the concentration in mol of Cd<sup>2+</sup>?

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cd</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>Fe</mtext><mfenced> <mi>s</mi> </mfenced><mo>→</mo><mtext>Cd</mtext><mfenced> <mi>s</mi> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mo>;</mo><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mi>o</mi> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.037</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>
 
Given: log 18 = 1.25
Report your answer upto two decimal places.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.05**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>Cd</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>Cd</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>At eqm.</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>-</mo> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>-</mo> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>At equilibrium, E</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0.0591</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>log</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cd</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

        <math> <mrow> <mtext>0.037</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0.0591</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>log</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

              <math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇒</mo> <mtext>0.947 M</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cd</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.053 M</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow></msup></math>
and <math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Al</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>3+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Al </mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.66</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>
Reduction potential of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Al</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>3+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Al</mtext></mrow> </math> couple at <math> <mrow> <mtext>pH</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>12</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.07&#x00A0;V</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.25&#x00A0;V</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.07&#x00A0;V</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.25</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.25</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
at <math> <mrow> <mtext>pH</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>12</mtext><mo>⇒</mo><mtext>pOH</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>14</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>12</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>OH</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.01</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>0.01</mn><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>30</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1.66</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>30</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.66</mtext><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>30</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.66</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.59</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.25</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

All the energy released from the reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mtext>193 kJ mo</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> is used for oxidizing <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> as <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
Under standard conditions, the number of moles of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> oxidized when one mole of X is converted to Y is [F = 96500 C <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> ]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>193</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.25</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

So No. of moles of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> oxidized by 1 moles of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A 250 mL sample of a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> is electrolysed with a current of 96.5 amp; if the remaining concentration of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> ion is 0.1 M, the duration of electrolysis is
given : atomic mass of Cr = 52

**Options:**

- **(A)** 75 sec
- **(B)** 150 sec
- **(C)** 225 sec
- **(D)** 25 sec

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 75 sec**

**Detailed Solution:**

Number of moles of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> before electrolysis <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo> </mo><mo>(</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>1000</mn></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.05</mn></math>
Number of moles of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> after electrolysed <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.025</mn></math>
Number of moles of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> electrolysed <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.05</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.025</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.025</mn></math>
Mass of chromium electrolysis <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.025</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>52</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1.3</mn></math> g
Equivalent mass of chromium <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mtext>Atomic mass</mtext><mtext>Valency of ions</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>52</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>17.33</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>ItE</mi><mn>96500</mn></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>96</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>17</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>33</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>75.14</mn></math> sec
<math><mo>≈</mo><mn>75</mn></math> sec.

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>-voltameter, mass deposited in <math><mn>30</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> is 'm' g. If the time-current graph is shown in the following figure:

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image1-852478df-0221-41f8-a787-dd761c07dac3.jpg" style="height: 177px; width: 240px;" />

What is the electrochemical equivalent of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>63.5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>30</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c = </mi><mo>Area of current-time curve</mo></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow> </math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="bold"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>100</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3000</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Consider an electrochemical cell: <math> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext> A</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>n+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq, 2 M</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext> B</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2n+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq, 1 M</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mtext> B</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math>. The value of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math> for the cell reaction is twice that of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math> at <math> <mrow> <mtext>300 </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow> </math> If the emf of the cell is zero, the <math><mo>∆</mo><msup><mrow><math> <mtext>S</mtext> </math> </mrow><mrow><mo>⊝</mo></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi> </mi><math> <mtext>J</mtext> </math> <mi></mi><math> <mtext>K</mtext> </math> <mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mi> </mi><math> <mtext>m</mtext> </math> <math> <mtext>o</mtext> </math> <msup><mrow><math> <mtext>l</mtext> </math> </mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo></math> of the cell reaction per mole of <math> <mtext>B</mtext> </math> formed at <math> <mrow> <mtext>300</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> is ____
(Given: <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0.7</mn><mo>,</mo><math> <mtext>R</mtext> </math> </mrow></mrow></math> <math> <mrow> <mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>8</mtext><mtext>.3</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>J</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo>.</mo></mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mtext>H,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>S</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mtext>G</mtext> </math> are enthalpy, entropy and Gibbs energy, respectively.)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **-11.60**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>A(s)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>|</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+n</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>(aq</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>||</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>B</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+2n</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>(aq</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>|</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>B(s)</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>2ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>Cell</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Rx</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>A</mtext><mo>→</mo><msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+n</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>ne</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>B</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+2n</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>ne</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><mtext>B(s)</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>A(s)</mtext><mo>+</mo><munder> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>B</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+2n</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M</mtext></mrow> </munder> <mtext>(aq)</mtext><mo>→</mo><munder> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+n</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M</mtext></mrow> </munder> <mtext>(aq)</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>B(s)</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mi>o</mi> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>RT</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>ln</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+n</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>B</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+2n</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>ΔG</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>RT</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>ln</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+n</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mtext> </mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>B</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>+2n</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>RT</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>ln</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>2</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>RT</mtext><mtext>ln4</mtext></mrow> </math>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Suppose that gold is being plated on to another metal in an electrolytic cell. The half-cell reaction producing the Au(s) is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>A</mtext><mtext>u</mtext><mtext>C</mtext><msubsup><mtext>l</mtext><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo></msubsup><msup><mtext> +3e</mtext><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>A</mtext><mtext>u</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mtext>s</mtext></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mtext>l</mtext><mo>-</mo></msup></math> . If a 0.30 A current runs for 15.00 minute, what mass of Au(s) will be plated, assume all the electrons are used in the reduction of <math><mtext>A</mtext><mtext>u</mtext><mtext>C</mtext><msubsup><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> ? The Faraday constant is 96485 <math><mrow><mrow><mtext>C</mtext></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mtext>m</mtext><mtext>o</mtext><mtext>l</mtext></mrow></mrow></math> and molar mass of Au is 197.

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.184 g
- **(B)** 0.551 g
- **(C)** 1.84 g
- **(D)** 0.613 g

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 0.184 g**

**Detailed Solution:**

Mass of Au deposited = Number of Faraday passed <math><mo>×</mo></math> Eq. mass

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.30</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>96485</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>197</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.184</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>g</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The density of copper is 8.94 g <math><mtext>m</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> . Find the charge needed to plate an area of <math><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> to a thickness of <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> cm using a <math><mtext>CuS</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> solution as electrolyte (atomic weight of Cu = 63.6 g/mol).

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2.7</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>8.8</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>18.3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1.7</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2.7</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math> 

or  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>.</mo></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ρ</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8.94</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>63.6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2.7</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> .

</details>
