### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The voltage of the cell consisting of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Li</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></math> electrodes is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5.92</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> at standard condition at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>298</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> . What is the voltage if the electrolyte consists of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>LiF</mi></math> .

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.693</mo><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.314</mo><msup><mi>JK</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>and</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>96500</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5.90&#160;</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5.937 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5.88 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4.9 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(E)** <math><mn>4.8 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>5.88 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Now, the cell reaction is

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Li</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mi>Li</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>

We know that,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi>cell</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi>cell</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>RT</mi><mi>nF</mi></mfrac><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi>cell</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>.303</mo><mi>RT</mi></mrow><mi>nF</mi></mfrac><mi>log</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi>Li</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5.92</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>298</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5.92</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5.92</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.035</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5.887&#160;</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

(given, <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mtext>F</mtext> <mtext>R</mtext> </mfrac> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>=11500 K V</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>,</mtext></mrow> </math> where <math> <mtext>F</mtext> </math> is the Faraday constant and <math> <mtext>R</mtext> </math> is the gas constants, <math> <mrow> <mtext>ln</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>10</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.30</mtext></mrow> </math> )

- **(A)** <math><mrow>  <mo>-</mo>  <mn>1</mn>  <mo>&times;</mo>  <mn>1</mn>  <msup>          <mn>0</mn>      <mrow>        <mo>-</mo>        <mn>2</mn>      </mrow>  </msup>  <msup>          <mo>JK</mo>        <mn>-1</mn>  </msup></mrow></math>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The pressure of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> required to make the potential of hydrogen electrode zero in pure water at 298 K is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> Hydrogen electrode <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> at <math><msup><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> (for Pure water)

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the following reduction processes

<math><mtext>Z</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>n</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>Zn</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>76 V</mtext></math>

<math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>Ca</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>87 V</mtext></math>

<math><mtext>M</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>Mg</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>36 V</mtext></math>

<math><mtext>N</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>i</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>Ni</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>25 V</mtext></math>

The reduction power of the metals increases in the order

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Reducing power <math><mo>∝</mo></math> Tendency to undergo oxidation
<math><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext>reduction potential values</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
So, reducing power decreases as <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the following cell reaction

 <math><mtext>2Fe</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>4</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>aq</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mtext>2F</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>aq</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> 

 <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>67 V</mtext></math> 

At  <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>F</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0.1</mn></math>  atm and  <math><mtext>pH</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> , the cell potential at  <math><msup><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>  is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.77 V
- **(B)** 1.57 V
- **(C)** 1.87 V
- **(D)** 1.47 V

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 1.57 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mtext>2Fe</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>4</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>aq</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>→</mo><mtext>2F</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>e</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>aq</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>  (no. of moles of electrons involved)

From Nernst equation,

 <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mrow></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>1.67</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log   log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>0.1</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>67</mtext><mo>-</mo><mn>0.106</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mtext>57 V</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Reduction potentials of four elements <math> <mrow> <mtext>P,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Q,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>R,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>S</mtext></mrow> </math> is <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.90</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>, <math> <mrow> <mtext>0.34</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>, <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.76</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>. The decreasing order of reducing power is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Reducing power, i.e. the tendency to lose electrons increases as the reduction potential decreases

</details>

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Given that : <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.23</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> ,

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mtext>S</mtext><msubsup><mrow><mtext>O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2.05</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>B</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>r</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mtext>B</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>r</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.09</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>A</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>u</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mtext>Au</mtext></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1.4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

The strongest oxidizing agent is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Oxidizing power  <math><mo>∝</mo></math>  Tendency to undergo reduction  <math><mo>∝</mo></math>  reduction potential value.

 <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

---

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

During an electrolysis of conc <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math>, peroxydisulphuric acid <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> form in an equimolar amount. The moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> that will be formed simultaneously will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** Thrice that of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** Twice that of  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** Equal to that of  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** Half of the of  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Thrice that of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
For 1 mole each of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> , no of mole of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> = 6 mole <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
So 6 mole <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
Hence it produces 3 moles <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>H</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate the standard electrode potential <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> for

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>

From the given standard electrode potentials value of two half cells

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo> </mo><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mtext>0.440</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo> </mo><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mtext>0.036</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>+&#x00A0;0</mtext><mtext>.772</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>&#x2013;&#x00A0;0</mtext><mtext>.404</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>+&#x00A0;0</mtext><mtext>.404</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>&#x2013;&#x00A0;0</mtext><mtext>.476</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow>  <mtext>+&#x00A0;0</mtext><mtext>.772</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> .....(i)

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>×</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mtext>0.440</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mtext>0.880</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> .....(ii)

<math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x0394;G</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mtext>F</mtext><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mfenced>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.036</mtext></mrow>  </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.108</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>F</mtext></mrow></math>

On subtracting equation (i) from (ii)

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>

<math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x0394;G</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.108</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>F</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.880</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>F</mtext><mo>=</mo><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.772</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>F</mtext></mrow></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math> for the reaction <math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>0.772</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mtext>=+</mtext><mo> </mo><mtext>0.772</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

</details>

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Electrolysis of hot aqueous solution of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> gives <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> , i.e., sodium perchlorate,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>↑</mo></math>
How many Faraday are required to obtain <math><mn>1000 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of sodium perchlorate? Report your answer by rounding it up to nearest whole number.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **66**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equivalent mass of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mtext>Molecular mass</mtext><mtext>Change in oxidation state of chloride</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>122.5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>15.13</mn></math>
Number of Faraday = Number of equivalent of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> formed
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15.13</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>66</mn></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What will be standard cell potential of galvanic cell with the following reaction?

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>2Cr</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>3Cd</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msubsup> <mo>→</mo><msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>2Cr</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>3+</mtext></mrow> </msubsup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>3Cd</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </msub> </mrow> </math>

[Given: <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.74</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> and <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.40</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> ]

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.74 V
- **(B)** 1.14 V
- **(C)** 0.34 V
- **(D)** -0.34 V

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 0.34 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cd</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Cd</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>−</mo><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cr</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>3+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Cr</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>0.40</mn><mo>−</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.74</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.34</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

When <math><mtext>X</mtext></math> amperes of current is passed through molten <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> for 96.5 s. 0.09 g of aluminium is deposited. What is the value of X?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 10 A
- **(B)** 20 A
- **(C)** 30 A
- **(D)** 40 A

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 10 A**

**Detailed Solution:**

Current <math><mtext>i</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mo>?</mo></math>

Equivalent weight of <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">A</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>

<math><mtext>w</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext>Z</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext>i</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext>t</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">o</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">r</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mtext>w</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>Z</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext>t</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.09</mn><mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn><mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mn>96.5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

= 10 A

</details>

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Given
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><msup><mi>Cl</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.36</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><msup><mi>Cr</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>/</mo><mi>Cr</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.74</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><msub><mi>Cr</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>7</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>/</mo><msup><mi>Cr</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.33</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><msubsup><mi>MnO</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo></msubsup><mo>/</mo><msup><mi>Mn</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.51</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
Among the following, the strongest reducing agent is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Mn</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Cr</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Cl</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cr</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cr</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

According to the given data standard reduction potential of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cr</mi></math> is lowest. Lower the standard  reduction potential, stronger the reducing agent. Therfore <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cr</mi></math> is the strongest reducing agent.

</details>

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A current strength of <math> <mrow> <mn>0.965</mn></mrow> </math> amperes is passed through excess fused <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> for <math> <mn>5</mn> </math> hours. How many litres of chlorine will be liberated at STP ? <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>96500</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 2.016
- **(B)** 1.008
- **(C)** 11.2
- **(D)** 20.16

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 20.16**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>F</mtext><mo>→</mo><mn>11.2</mn><msub> <mrow> <mtext> L Cl</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext> at STP</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9.65</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.8</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext>Volume</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>of Cl</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext> at STP</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>1.8</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>11.2</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>L</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>20</mtext><mtext>.16</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>L</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A cell contains two hydrogen electrodes. The negative electrode is in contact with a solution of <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> M hydrogen ion. The EMF of the cell is 0.118 V at 298 K. The concentration of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> ion at the positive electrode is <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup></math> ,The value of 'x' is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>/</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> (0.1 M) half cell is connected to a <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>/</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> (1 bar)/Pt half cell and e.m.f. is found to be 0.7714 V. If <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.535</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> , find the pH of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> half cell.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

The cell reaction is 
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>  
Here <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> (n = number of electons involved ) 
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mn>0.7714</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.535</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>[</mo><mn>0.1</mn><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
On solving <math><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo></math>  
So <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q65. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate standard free energy change for the reaction <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>

Given: <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.80</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 308.08 kJ
- **(B)** 154.4 kJ
- **(C)** 77.2 kJ
- **(D)** – 154.4 kJ

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 154.4 kJ**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.80</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.80</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>154.4 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

</details>

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Determine the value of the equilibrium constant for the reaction
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.54</mn><mtext>V</mtext></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5.01</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** 18.30
- **(C)** <math><mn>1.7</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>54</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1.9</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>5.01</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The relation between equilibrium constant (K) and <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> is given as
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0.059</mn><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
here, n = 2
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0.54</mn><mtext>V</mtext><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.059</mn><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1.08</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>18.3</mn></math>
On taking antilog
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5.01</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

An electrolytic cell is constructed for preparing hydrogen. For an average current of 2 ampere in the circuit, the time in seconds required to produce 450 mL of hydrogen at NTP is approximately

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1930**

**Detailed Solution:**

22400 mL <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> at STP <math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>mol</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
So 450 ml <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> at STP
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>450</mn><mtext>mL</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>22400</mn><mtext>mL</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>mol</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.02</mn></math> mol
Electrode reaction
<math><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext></mrow></munder><mo>→</mo><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext>mol</mtext></mrow></munder></math>
The liberation of 1 mol <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> requires <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mtext>C</mtext></math>
So the liberation of 0.02 mol <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> requires <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.02</mn><mtext>C</mtext></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3860</mn></math> C
Time required to produce 0.02 mol <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> , <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3860</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>A</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1930</mn><mtext> sec</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> at equilibrium, the concentration of A is <math><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> B is 0.15 M and C is 0.05 M. The <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo></math> for the hydrolysis of A at 300 K is <math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> kJ/mole. The value of X is?
Report your answer by rounding it upto nearest integer.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

As <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced></mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.15</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.05</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msub><mi>log</mi><mn>10</mn></msub><mo>⁡</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2.303</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>300</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
On solving <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4.99</mn></math> kJ/M
<math><mo>≃</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math> kJ/M
So <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Find out <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo></math> for <math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mtext>F</mtext>   <mtext>-</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo><mfrac>   <mtext>1</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </mfrac>  <msub>   <mtext>F</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mtext>e</mtext>   <mo>&#x2212;</mo>  </msup>  </mrow></math>; if <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>F</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>2e</mtext></mrow>   <mo>&#x2212;</mo>  </msup>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>2F</mtext></mrow>   <mo>&#x2212;</mo>  </msup>  </mrow></math>; <math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mtext>E</mtext>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.7&#x00A0;V</mtext></mrow></math>.

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.35 V
- **(B)** -1.35 V
- **(C)** 2.7 V
- **(D)** -2.7 V

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) -2.7 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>°</mo></math>  values do not depend upon stoichiometry of change but depends upon the direction, 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><msubsup><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>

</details>

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a Cu-voltameter, mass deposited in 30s is m gm. If the time-current graph is shown in the following figure

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/quesnnjjf.JPG" style="width: 200px; height: 139px;" />

What is the electrochemical equivalent of Cu?

**Options:**

- **(A)** m/2
- **(B)** m/3
- **(C)** m/4
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>63.5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) m/3**

**Detailed Solution:**

Total charge passed in 30 sec.

 <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>3000</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mtext> s</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext>3C</mtext></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> 

So  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Given

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cr</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <mtext>Cr</mtext> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>.</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mtext> V;  </mtext><msubsup><mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>MnO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Mn</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>.</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>1 V</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mrow> <mtext>E</mtext><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext> <msub> <mtext>r</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msubsup> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>7</mn> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> </mrow> </msubsup> <mo>/</mo> <mtext>C</mtext> <msup> <mtext>r</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mi>.</mi> <mn>3</mn> <mn>3</mn> <mtext> V</mtext> <mtext>;  </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <msub><mtext>Cl</mtext><mo>2</mo></msub> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Cl</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>.</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>6 V</mn> </mrow> </math>

Based on the data given above,strongest oxidising agent will be :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math ><mrow>  <msup>          <mtext>Mn</mtext>      <mrow>        <mn>2</mn>        <mo>+</mo>      </mrow>  </msup></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>MnO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow>  <msup>          <mtext>Cl</mtext>        <mo>-</mo>  </msup></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Cr</mtext> <mrow><mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>MnO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Strongest oxidising agent has the highest standard reduction potential

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>MnO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> <mo>-</mo> </msubsup> <mo>/</mo> <msup> <mtext>Mn</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>5</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>V</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q72. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Four different solutions containing <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>M</mtext></mrow> </math> each of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> are being electrolysed by using inert electrodes. In how many samples, metal ions would be deposited at cathode?
[Given: <math><msub><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1.00</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> , <math><msub><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.34</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3.03</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math> ]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

SRP of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> is more than SRP of lithium. 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> will be reduced at cathode in case of <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> aq. Solution

</details>

### Q73. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let a fully charged lead storage battery contains 1.5 L of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M </mi><msub><mtext>H</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>4</mn></msub></math> . What will be the concentration of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> in the battery after 2.5 ampere current is drawn from the battery for 6 hour?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 4.626 M
- **(B)** 0.1865 M
- **(C)** 0.373 M
- **(D)** 9.627 M

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 4.626 M**

**Detailed Solution:**

Number of moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> before electrolysis
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>7.5</mn></math>
Write cell rection <math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><munder><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
For 2 moles of electrons
2 moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> are used
Moles of electrons used <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2.5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3600</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.56</mn></math>
Hence, moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> used <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.56</mn></math>
Remaining mol of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>7.5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.56</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6.94</mn></math>
Final molarity <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>1000</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6.94</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1000</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>1000</mn></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4.626</mn></math>

</details>

### Q74. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

At <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>30</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></msub></math> will be
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/b4ff17af-33b1-4063-98d9-25809ec024ba.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.48333in;height:1.26667in" />

**Options:**

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>42</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.236</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>418</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> V</mi></math>

</details>
