### Q75. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate the cell potential of following cell

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.1</mn><mtext> bar</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mtext>BOH</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mtext>0.1M</mtext></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>HA</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mtext>0.1M</mtext></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> bar</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mtext>Pt</mtext></math>

Given <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.39 V
- **(B)** 0.36 V
- **(C)** 0.93 V
- **(D)** 0.63 V

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 0.39 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msubsup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>×</mo><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msubsup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>×</mo><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></msub></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>0.1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math>

<math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math>

<math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></msub></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>0.1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math>

So <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>0.1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math> <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><mn>0.</mn><mtext>39</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>.

</details>

### Q76. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

E.M.F. diagram for some ions is given as
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mover accent="false"><mo>→</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2.20</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mover accent="false"><mo>→</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.77</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mover accent="false"><mo>→</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.445</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math>
Determine the value of <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.84 V
- **(B)** 1.42 V
- **(C)** 1.3 V
- **(D)** 2.0 V

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 1.84 V**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math> <math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2.20</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> …(i)
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math> <math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.77</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> …(ii)
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math> <math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.445</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> …(iii)
By equation (i) and (ii)
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math> <math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math>
Hence <math><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2.20</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.77</mn><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
So <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1.84</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1.84</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

</details>

### Q77. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What will be the volume of O<sub>2</sub> Liberated at NTP by passing 5 A current For 193 Sec. through acidified water.

**Options:**

- **(A)** 56 mL
- **(B)** 112 mL
- **(C)** 158 mL
- **(D)** 965 mL

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 56 mL**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>W</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>i</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>t</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>E</mtext></mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

    <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>3</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∵</mo> </mrow> </math>  At NTP, volume of 32 g O<sub>2</sub> = 22400 mL

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo> </mrow> </math> Volume of 0.08 g O<sub>2</sub> <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>= 56 mL</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q78. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a cell that utilizes the reaction
<math> <mrow> <mtext>Zn(s)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>2H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mtext>(aq)</mtext><mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext>(aq)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow> </math>
addition of H<sub>2</sub>SO<sub>4</sub> to cathode compartment,

**Options:**

- **(A)** Lowers the E and shifts equilibrium to the left
- **(B)** Lowers the E and shifts the equilibrium to the right
- **(C)** Increases the E and shifts the equilibrium to the right
- **(D)** Increases the E and shifts the equilibrium to the left

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Increases the E and shifts the equilibrium to the right**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <mtext>Zn(s)</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>2H</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>+</mtext>  </msup>  <mtext>(aq)</mtext><mo>&#x2192;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow>  </msup>  <mtext>(aq)</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>H</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>E</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x00A0;cell</mtext></mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msubsup>   <mtext>E</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x00A0;cell</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msubsup>  <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.059</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </mfrac>  <mtext>&#x00A0;log</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00A0;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msup>       <mrow>        <mtext>Zn</mtext></mrow>       <mrow>        <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow>      </msup>      </mrow>    </mfenced><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00D7;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>H</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msup>     <mrow>      <mfenced close="]" open="[">       <mrow>        <msup>         <mtext>H</mtext>         <mtext>+</mtext>        </msup>        </mrow>      </mfenced></mrow>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msup>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
On adding <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>SO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>, the <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> will increase therefore <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext> cell</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> will also increase and the equilibrium will shift towards the right.

</details>

---

### Q79. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

How many of these metals can displace <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> easily from acids. 
Fe, Mg, Al, Cu, Ag, Au, Zn

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

Fe, Mg, Al and Zn can easily displace <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> from acids due to more reactivity as they are having more <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> than hydrogen. 
Cu, Ag, Au can not do it as they have low <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> than hydrogen.

</details>

### Q80. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate the time required for complete decomposition of one mole of water using <math> <mn>2</mn> </math> ampere current. .

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>9650&#x00A0;sec</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>96500 sec</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.93</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00D7;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>10</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>5</mtext>  </msup>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>sec</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>38600&#x00A0;sec</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <mtext>96500 sec</mtext></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
One mole of <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext></mrow> </math> need <math> <mtext>2</mtext> </math> moles of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mtext>s</mtext></mrow> </math> for electrolysis.
So, <math> <mrow> <mtext>Q</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>I</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>t</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x21D2;</mo><mtext>t</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mtext>Q</mtext>   <mtext>I</mtext>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>96500</mn></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext>t</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>96500</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>sec</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q81. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>2e</mtext></mrow> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><mtext>Cu;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>log</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2+</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>vs</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>red</mtext></mrow> </msub> </mrow> </math> graph is of the type as shown in figure where <math> <mrow> <mtext>OA</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0.34</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math> then electrode potential of the half cell of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>0.1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>)</mo></math> will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>0.34</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0.34</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.0591</mn></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0.34</mn></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>0.34</mn></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>0.34</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.0591</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><mi>Cu</mi><mo>/</mo><msup><mi>Cu</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mrow><mi>Cu</mi><mo>/</mo><msup><mi>Cu</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mn>0</mn></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>059</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mi>log</mi><mfenced close="]" open="["><msup><mi>Cu</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mfenced></math></p><p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>log</mi><mfenced close="]" open="["><msup><mi>Cu</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> i.e., <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><msup><mi>Cu</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi *Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The cell, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>(</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>1.10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> was allowed to be completely discharged at 298K. The relative concentration of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup></math> . The value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> is:
( <math><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.059</mn></math> Round off your answer up to one decimal)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **37**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ; when cell is completely discharged
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></msup></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1.1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1.1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.059</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>37.3</mn></math>
` <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>37.3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>37.3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q83. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Three Faraday of electricity is passed through aqueous solutions of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> kept in three vessels using inert electrodes. The ratio in mol in which the metals Ag, Ni and Cr will be deposited is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1:2:3
- **(B)** 3:2:1
- **(C)** 6:3:2
- **(D)** 2:3:6

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 6:3:2**

**Detailed Solution:**

(i) <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> mol = 1F</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></munder></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> mol</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mtext> mol</mtext></mrow></munder></mrow></munder></math>

(ii) <math><munder><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Ni</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> mol = 2F</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></munder></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> mol</mtext></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mtext> mol</mtext></mrow></munder></mrow></munder></math>

(iii) <math xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'> <mrow>  <munder>   <mrow>    <msup>     <mrow>      <mtext>Cr</mtext></mrow>     <mrow>      <mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow>    </msup>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mtext>aq</mtext></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>1</mn><mtext>&#x00A0;mol&#x00A0;=&#x00A0;3F</mtext></mrow>  </munder>  <mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup>   <mtext>e</mtext>   <mo>&#x2212;</mo>  </msup>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo><munder>   <mrow>    <mtext>Cr</mtext><mfenced>     <mtext>s</mtext>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>1</mn><mtext>&#x00A0;mol</mtext></mrow>  </munder>  </mrow></math>

The required ratio of moles of Ag, Ni and Cr is

3 mol Ag: 3/2 mol Ni : 1 mol Cr or 6 mol Ag : 3 mol Ni : 2 mol Cr.

</details>

### Q84. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

EMF of the following cell is <math><mn>0.6</mn></math> volt.

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.01</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.001</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> is expressed as <math><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> is

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.06</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **15.00**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.001</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mn>0.001</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mn>0.6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.06</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q85. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A silver cup is plated with silver by passing 965 C of electricity. The amount of Ag deposited is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 107.89 g
- **(B)** 9.89 g
- **(C)** 1.0002 g
- **(D)** 1.08 g

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 1.08 g**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⟶</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

∵       96500 C are required to deposite Ag = 108 g 

∴       965 C are required to deposite Ag 

<math><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>108</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>96500</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi> </mi><mn>965</mn><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1.08</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

</details>

### Q86. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The standard reduction potential for <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mtext>/Cu</mtext></mrow> </math> is <math><mo>+</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0.34</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>V</mtext><mo>.</mo></math> Calculate the reduction potential at <math> <mrow> <mtext>pH</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow> </math> for the above couple. <math> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>sp</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> of</mtext> <mtext> Cu</mtext><msub> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>OH</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>0.22</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>V</mtext></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>+</mo><mn>0.22</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>V</mtext></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>0.44</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>V</mtext></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>+</mo><mn>0.44</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>V</mtext></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>0.22</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>V</mtext></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

When <math> <mrow> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>p</mtext><mtext>H</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>14, </mn><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow><msup><mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>and</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mtext>O</mtext><msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi> M</mi></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext><mtext>p</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext><msup> <mtext>u</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><msup> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mtext>O</mtext><msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext><msup> <mtext>u</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mtext>O</mtext><msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

The half cell reaction

<math> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext><msup> <mtext>u</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><mtext>C</mtext><mtext>u</mtext></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>E</mtext><mo>=</mo><msup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mtext>O</mtext> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.059</mn></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mi>log</mi><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext><msup> <mtext>u</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.34</mn><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.059</mn></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mi>log</mi><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>0.22 </mn><mtext>V</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q87. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The standard EMF. of a cell, involving one electron change is found to be <math> <mrow> <mtext>0.591 V</mtext> </mrow> </math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>25</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">℃</mi></math>. The equilibrium constant of the reaction is <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>10</mtext></mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>. Find the value of <math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math>.
<math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mtext>96500 C  equivalent</mtext><mn>-1</mn> </msup> <mtext>, R</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mtext>8.314 J K</mtext> <mn>-1</mn> </msup> <msup> <mtext> mol</mtext> <mn>-1</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **10**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ΔG</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>ΔG</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.591</mo></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mfrac><mi>logQ</mi></math>

at equilibrium

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ΔG</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi>cell</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><mo>,</mo><mi>ΔG</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.591</mo></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mi>logQ</mi></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext> cell</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msubsup> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext> cell</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.0591</mtext></mrow> <mtext>n</mtext> </mfrac> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>log K</mtext></mrow> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.591</mtext><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.0591</mtext></mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mfrac> <mo> </mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>log K</mtext></mrow> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x21D2;</mo><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.591</mtext><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.0591</mtext><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>log&#x00A0;K</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  </mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒    </mo><msub><mtext>log K</mtext><mtext>c</mtext></msub><mo> = </mo><mfrac><mtext>0.591</mtext><mtext>0.0591</mtext></mfrac><mo> = </mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴     </mo> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mo> = </mo> <mtext>antilog 10</mtext> <mo> = </mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mtext>10</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>.

</details>

### Q88. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A current of <math> <mrow> <mtext>5</mtext><mtext>.0</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>A</mtext></mrow> </math> flows for <math> <mrow> <mtext>4</mtext><mtext>.0</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>h</mtext></mrow> </math> through an electrolytic cell containing a molten salt of metal M. This results in deposition of <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.25</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> </math> of the metal <math> <mtext>M</mtext> </math> at the cathode. The oxidation state of <math> <mtext>M</mtext> </math> in the molten salt is <math> <mrow> <mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </math>. the value of '<math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math>' is (<math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Faraday</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>96000</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>C</mtext></mrow> </math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>I</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>5.0</mn><mtext> A</mtext><mo>,</mo><mtext> t</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>4.0</mn><mtext> h</mtext></mrow> </math>
Moles of metal deposited <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5.0</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn></math> coulombs
Charge in faradays
<math> <mrow> <mtext>Q</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>60</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>96000</mn></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.75</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>F</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> moles of metal will be deposited by <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> F charge required
So, for 1 mole, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>
Oxidation <math><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q89. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What will be the emf of the given cell  <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>Pt | H</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>(P</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>) | H</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>(aq) | H</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>(P</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>) | Pt ?</mtext> </mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn></mn><mn></mn><mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext>&thinsp;</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext>&thinsp;</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn></mn><mn></mn><mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext><mtext>t</mtext><mrow><mo>|</mo> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mo>|</mo></mrow><msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mrow><mo>|</mo> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mo>|</mo></mrow><mtext>P</mtext><mtext>t</mtext></mrow> </math>

Both the electrodes share the same <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext><mo>:</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/nsa_ghaskjgasdgasdgas.png" style="width: 200px; height: 106px;" />

Applying Nernst equation

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>E</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>c</mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>l</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext mathvariant="script">l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>a</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext mathvariant="script">l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext><mtext>T</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>F</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext mathvariant="script">l</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q90. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An electric charge of 5 Faradays is passed through three electrolytes <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> solution. The grams of each metal liberated at cathode will be
[Atomic weight; Fe - 56 g/mol, Cu - 63.5 g/mol, Ag - 108 g/mol]

**Options:**

- **(A)** Ag = 10.8 g, Cu = 12.7 g, Fe = 1.11 g
- **(B)** Ag = 540 g, Cu = 367.5 g, Fe = 325 g
- **(C)** Ag = 108 g, Cu = 63.5 g, Fe = 56 g
- **(D)** Ag = 540 g, Cu = 158.8 g, Fe = 93.3 g

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Ag = 540 g, Cu = 158.8 g, Fe = 93.3 g**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><mo>≡</mo><mtext> 1F</mtext></mrow></munder><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><mo>≡</mo><mtext> 2F</mtext></mrow></munder><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math> 

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><mo>≡</mo><mtext> 3F</mtext></mrow></munder><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> 

Grams of Ag liberated  <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>108</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>540</mn><mtext> g</mtext></math> 

Grams of Cu liberated  <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>63.5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>158.75</mn><mtext> g</mtext></math> 

Grams of Fe liberated  <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>56</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>93.3</mn><mtext> g</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q91. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Metals which will not evolve <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> gas with <math> <mrow> <mtext>dil</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>HCl</mtext></mrow> </math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** Cu, Ag, Au
- **(B)** Cu, Zn, Al
- **(C)** Fe, Ag, Pt
- **(D)** Hg, Mg, Pt

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Cu, Ag, Au**

**Detailed Solution:**

The metals whose reduction potentials are greater than the reduction potential of Hydrogen do not liberate <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> gas.
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>+</mtext> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>−</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>;E</mtext></mrow> <mtext>ο</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.00</mn><mtext> V</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Au</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>3</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mo>–</mo> </msup> <mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>  Au             E</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>1.50</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>V </mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext> e</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>–</mo><mtext> </mtext></mrow> </msup> <mo>→</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Ag                   E</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mtext>  </mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>0.799</mn><mtext> V</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow>  </msup>  <mtext>&#x00A0;</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>&#x00A0;</mtext><mn>2</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mtext>e</mtext>   <mo>&#x2013;</mo>  </msup>  <mtext>&#x00A0;&#x00A0;</mtext><mo>&#x2192;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>Cu&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;&#x00A0;E</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mtext>&#x00A0;</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>&#x00A0;</mtext><mn>0.34</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>V</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

---
